      "content": "I am your supervisor and you are a super intelligent AI Assistant whose job is to achieve my day-to-day tasks completely autonomously.\n\nTo do this, you will need to interact with app/s (e.g., spotify, venmo etc) using their associated APIs on my behalf. \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"query\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Search query string.\",\n   \"default\": \"\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"user_email\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"If passed, only transactions between you and user with this email address will be shown.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value is email address\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\": \"1500-01-01\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_created_at\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum created_at datetime to filter transactions in YYYY-MM-DD format.\",\n   \"default\": \"3000-01-01\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_like_count\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum like count to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_like_count\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum like count to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 9223372036854775807,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"min_amount\",\n   \"type\": \"number\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Minimum amount to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value > 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"max_amount\",\n   \"type\": \"number\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Maximum amount to filter transactions.\",\n   \"default\": 9223372036854775807,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value > 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"private\",\n   \"type\": \"boolean\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Filter transactions by privacy.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"direction\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Filter transactions by direction of the transaction (sent or received). Will skip filtering if not passed.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value in ['sent', 'received']\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_index\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The index of the page to return.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_limit\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The maximum number of results to return per page.\",\n   \"default\": 5,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 1.0, <= 20.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"sort_by\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The attribute to sort the transactions by prefixed with +/- to reflect ascending/descending. Valid attributes: created_at, like_count and amount. If both query and sort_by are given and non-empty, results will be first ranked by query relevance, then paginated, and will then be sorted by the given attribute within each page.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"response_schemas\": {\n  \"success\": [\n   {\n    \"transaction_id\": 1,\n    \"amount\": 0.0,\n    \"description\": \"string\",\n    \"created_at\": \"2019-01-01T00:00:00\",\n    \"updated_at\": \"2019-01-01T00:00:00\",\n    \"private\": true,\n    \"like_count\": 1,\n    \"payment_card_digits\": \"string\",\n    \"comment_count\": 1,\n    \"sender\": {\n     \"name\": \"string\",\n     \"email\": \"user@example.com\"\n    },\n    \"receiver\": {\n     \"name\": \"string\",\n     \"email\": \"user@example.com\"\n    }\n   }\n  ],\n  \"failure\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\"\n  }\n }\n}\n```",
      "role": "user"
    }
  ],

  "prompt_tokens": 17725,
  "raw_model_response": "I need to filter the transactions to only include those related to the dinner at Azure Harbor Bistro. I can use the `query` parameter with the search string \"Azure Harbor Bistro\" to find these transactions. I also need to filter the transactions to only include those that were sent by the manager or received by the manager. Let me check the `show_transactions` API parameters to understand how to do this.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_doc(app_name='venmo', api_name='show_transactions'))\n```",
